# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-28T12:24:54+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

## Wider reading

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Sat, 28 Nov 2026 08:07:00 -0300 — Interdição fraudulenta de idoso pode levar à perda de herança
  A luta por uma velhice mais digna ganhou mais um capítulo esta semana, com a aprovação de um projeto de Lei (PL) que impede o recebimento de herança por quem tiver promovido, de forma fraudulenta, processo contra pessoa idosa, alegando sua incapacidade civil, a chamada curatela abusiva. A proposta foi aprovada na Comissão de Constituição e Justiça (CCJ) da Câmara dos Deputados na terça-feira (24) e aguarda, agora, a votação em plenário. Notícias relacionadas: Falecido deixou dívidas? Saiba quem deve pagar. Conheça os instrumentos de planejamento sucessório. De autoria do deputado Eriberto Medeiros (PSB-PE), o PL 464/26 , já havia sido aprovado pela Comissão de Defesa dos Direitos do Idoso na forma do substitutivo do deputado Geraldo Resende (União-MS) e aguardava apenas a redação final. Pe
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/politica/noticia/2026-11/interdicao-fraudulenta-de-idoso-pode-levar-perda-de-heranca
- Fri, 27 Nov 2026 19:40:00 -0300 — Distribuidoras de energia oferecem descontos para renegociar dívidas
  As distribuidoras de energia elétrica iniciaram campanhas de renegociação de dívidas, com condições especiais para consumidores que buscam regularizar débitos com os recursos do décimo terceiro salário. A primeira parcela do benefício deve ser paga até a próxima segunda-feira (30). As campanhas oferecem condições de parcelamento e descontos, que variam conforme a empresa, e podem ajudar consumidores a evitar a negativação e até o corte de energia. O país tem mais de 40 distribuidoras, e as condições variam de acordo com a localidade, as características da dívida e o perfil do consumidor. Empresas participantes. Notícias relacionadas: Vendas do Tesouro Direto registram melhor outubro da história. Ferramenta gratuita reúne mudanças para MEIs e pequenos negócios. Contas do governo têm superáv
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/economia/noticia/2026-11/distribuidoras-de-energia-oferecem-descontos-para-renegociar-dividas
- Fri, 27 Nov 2026 18:50:00 -0300 — Inauguração da Árvore de Natal da Lagoa terá queima de fogos e drones
  Uma operação especial foi montada para a inauguração da tradicional Árvore de Natal da Lagoa, na zona sul do Rio de Janeiro, que este ano está completando 30 anos. Para o lançamento neste sábado (28), a partir das 17h, no Complexo Lagoon, haverá um concerto da Orquestra Petrobras Sinfônica, às 18h30. A árvore será acesa às 20h, com fogos e um show. A orquestra continua a apresentação até as 21h. Os telões instalados no Parque dos Patins e Cantagalo garantirão que o público em diferentes pontos da Lagoa acompanhe o espetáculo. Notícias relacionadas: Mostra no Recife destaca diversidade de filmes de animação africanos. TV Brasil: Sem Censura reúne Adriana Calcanhotto e Arnaldo Antunes. Mulheres ganham espaço na animação, mas ainda são minoria. Uma das novidades desse ano é uma estrela em 3D
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/cultura/noticia/2026-11/inauguracao-da-arvore-de-natal-da-lagoa-tera-queima-de-fogos-e-drones

  This week, I’ve sat in four different classrooms in Seoul, with young adults from all over the world, watching "Train to Busan" as part of our exploration of modern Korean society.   Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission - ohb.de Skip to content Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission Bremen, November 17, 2026 Low Earth Orbit (LEO) is becoming increasingly congested as the amount of space debris continues to grow. Thousands of defunct satellites and millions of debris fragments orbit Earth, increasing the risk of collisions. With the ClearSpace-1 mission (CS-1), Europe aims to demonstrate for the first time how a satellite can be captured and safely removed from orbit in a controlled manner. For this pioneering mission, the European Space Agency (ESA) has selected OHB System AG as prime contractor. The contract, valued at more than €102 million, was signed today at Space Tech Expo Bremen. &#8220;We are delighted
  https://www.ohb.de/en/news/ohb-awarded-eur-102-million-contract-by-esa-for-europes-first-active-space-debris-removal-mission

- undated — Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data This study is a decade-scale demonstration of the value of high-frequency imagery in monitoring and protecting dynamic shorelines.
  Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data HOME Publications Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data Austin Stone November 23, 2026 • 3 min read PlanetScope image of the Everglades coastline in Florida, captured May 15, 2025. © 2026, Planet Labs PBC. All Rights reserved. Publications Publications Satellite imagery is a critical resource in monitoring the health of coastal environments that are increasingly vulnerable to the impacts of climate and land use change. However, these ecosystems are so dynamic that many short-lived events can be missed between image captures by satellites that provide resolutions coarser than 10 meters with multiday revisit times, such as Landsat and Sentinel-2. While high-resolution aerial flights or drone surveys offer sub-meter-scale detail, they are costly, localized, and impossible to deploy systematically across entire national coas
  https://www.planet.com/pulse/monitoring-the-coasts-with-near-daily-satellite-data

By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days.   A luta por uma velhice mais digna ganhou mais um capítulo esta semana, com a aprovação de um projeto de Lei (PL) que impede o recebimento de herança por quem tiver promovido, de forma fraudulenta, processo contra pessoa idosa, alegando sua incapacidade civil, a chamada curatela abusiva. A proposta foi aprovada na Comissão de Constituição e Justiça (CCJ) da Câmara dos Deputados na terça-feira (24) e aguarda, agora, a votação em plenário. - Fri, 27 Nov 2026 19:40:00 -0300 — Distribuidoras de energia oferecem descontos para renegociar dívidas
  As distribuidoras de energia elétrica iniciaram campanhas de renegociação de dívidas, com condições especiais para consumidores que buscam regularizar débitos com os recursos do décimo terceiro salário. A primeira parcela do benefício deve ser paga até a próxima segunda-feira (30). As campanhas oferecem condições de parcelamento e descontos, que variam conforme a empresa, e podem ajudar consumidores a evitar a negativação e até o corte de energia. O país tem mais de 40 distribuidoras, e as condições variam de acordo com a localidade, as características da dívida e o perfil do consumidor. Empresas participantes. - Fri, 27 Nov 2026 18:50:00 -0300 — Inauguração da Árvore de Natal da Lagoa terá queima de fogos e drones
  Uma operação especial foi montada para a inauguração da tradicional Árvore de Natal da Lagoa, na zona sul do Rio de Janeiro, que este ano está completando 30 anos. Para o lançamento neste sábado (28), a partir das 17h, no Complexo Lagoon, haverá um concerto da Orquestra Petrobras Sinfônica, às 18h30. A árvore será acesa às 20h, com fogos e um show. A orquestra continua a apresentação até as 21h. Os telões instalados no Parque dos Patins e Cantagalo garantirão que o público em diferentes pontos da Lagoa acompanhe o espetáculo. De acordo com um novo relatório da consultoria TrendForce, os preços dos módulos DDR4 e DDR3 iniciaram uma trajetória de queda , levando a empresa de pesquisa a revisar para baixo suas projeções de aumento nos contratos de DRAM para 2027 . Continua após a publicidade Embora o cenário não indique que os componentes ficarão baratos imediatamente , os dados apontam para uma leve diminuição na crise de abastecimento. - undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/
- undated — TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies & Service Comparison
  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions. - undated — BlackSky Acquires Data Science Firm EpochGeo
  BlackSky Acquires Data Science Firm EpochGeo Skip to content BlackSky ($BKSY) announced its acquisition of geospatial data science firm EpochGeo yesterday, which will merge EpochGeo’s AI/ML capabilities with BlackSky’s existing constellation and analytics tools. There’s no shortage of imagery flowing into defense customers’ hands. But, to make it usable for quick decision-making, companies are working to shift from capturing pixels to automatically fusing electronic signals with overhead photos in real time. “BlackSky’s satellites and AI already show customers what is happening at the places that matter most to them,” BlackSky CEO and president Brian O’Toole told Payload via email. “EpochGeo adds the ability to tell them where they should be taking a closer look.”&nbsp; The target: Founded in 2018, EpochGeo builds software that fuses RF signals, publicly available data, and satellite ima
  https://payloadspace.com/blacksky-acquires-data-science-firm-epochgeo/
- undated — Cerberus to Acquire Airbus US Space & Defense
  Cerberus to Acquire Airbus US Space &amp; Defense Skip to content Cerberus Capital Management is acquiring the US space systems business of Airbus US Space &amp; Defense, the investment firm announced Tuesday. The acquisition is expected to close by the end of the year. The companies did not disclose the financial terms of the deal.&nbsp; Cerberus’ POV: Under the deal, the firm will acquire the defense and space subsidiary’s 200,000 square foot product facility near Kennedy Space Center. Cerberus intends to invest in the “engineering organization, product portfolio, and US supply chain to build a leading independent, US-owned satellite platform,” according to a press release. &#8220;With its strong flight track record, engineering depth, and production capability, the Business is well-positioned for growth as a trusted, independent merchant supplier of spacecraft,” Danil Subkhankulov, ma
  https://payloadspace.com/cerberus-to-acquire-airbus-us-space-defense/
- undated — Quantum Space Goes Public as SPACs Return
  Quantum Space Goes Public as SPACs Return Skip to content Quantum Space is the latest space company to go public this year, closing its merger with special purpose acquisition company (SPAC) Inflection Point Acquisition Corp. VI on Monday. The combined company is expected to start trading on the NYSE today under the ticker $QSPC. The deal brought in ~$300M of new capital at an implied pro forma equity value of ~$958M, down from the ~$1.2B Quantum predicted when it announced the merger in June. Quantum plans to pour the cash into production of its Ranger and Scout spacecraft, though Ranger has yet to fly.&nbsp; In June, CEO Jim Bridenstine said in a press conference that speed drove the choice of a SPAC over a traditional IPO. &#8220;We need to go as fast as possible to get these capabilities on-orbit as soon as possible,&#8221; he said.&nbsp; SPAC is back: For years, the word SPAC alone
  https://payloadspace.com/quantum-space-goes-public-as-spacs-return/

#### SpacePolicyOnline (space_press, en, newsroom)

- undated — FCC Approves Elon Musk’s One Million Orbital Data Centers
  FCC Approves Elon Musk&#8217;s One Million Orbital Data Centers &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com FCC Chairman Brendan Carr announced today that the FCC has approved Elon Musk&#8217;s request to launch one million satellites to low Earth orbit to serve as data centers in space. Called Starmind, they are in addition to the 42,000 Starlink broadband internet satellites SpaceX plans to deploy plus the military version, Starshield. The vast number of satellites is raising concerns about collisions, atmospheric effects of reentry, and the impact on optical and radio astronomy. SpaceX revealed plans to build one million Orbital Data Centers in January, but it&#8217;s not the only company with sights set on taking advantage of the continuous sunlight available in space to power ODCs. Luckily for him, a special treat will soon be on its way to the International Space Station. On November 29, as part of the NG-25 resupply mission, Northrop Grumman's Cygnus cargo spacecraft is scheduled to lift off from Cape Canaveral, Florida, carrying a little taste of home: poutine! Joshua will get to indulge in a unique kind of poutine, which is the result of a collaboration between the CSA , La Tablée des Chefs and Executive Chef Yves Lowe. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

- Thu, 26 Nov 2026 13:39:02 +0100 — Watch live: Gaia releases its largest-ever goldmine of cosmic data
  Join the European Space Agency and Gaia mission representatives on Wednesday 2 December for the unveiling of Gaia Data Release 4, the mission’s first principal data release.

- Wed, 25 Nov 2026 12:02:51 +0100 — Hera reveals stadium-sized asteroid crater from spacecraft impact
  Arriving at the site of humankind&#039;s first planetary defence experiment, the European Space Agency&#039;s Hera mission has found a miniature world of surprises.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/space-safety/hera/hera-reveals-stadium-sized-asteroid-crater-left-after-spacecraft-impact
- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 09:00:00 +0100 — Euclid breaks own quasar record by 40 million years
  The European Space Agency’s Euclid space telescope has discovered the most distant quasar to date: a rare 360 million-sun-mass galactic core seen just 630 million years after the Big Bang. The discovery breaks Euclid’s own previous record, announced only a few months ago[1.1], by peering back in time an additional 40 million years. It also raises a compelling question: how could such an enormous black hole have formed and grown so rapidly after the Big Bang?
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/science/euclid/euclid-breaks-own-quasar-record-by-40-million-years
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 19:00:00 +0100 — BepiColombo safely in Mercury orbit despite unexpected challenge
  On 21 November, starting at 18:24 UTC, BepiColombo achieved Mercury orbit insertion (MOI), the second major milestone of its planetary arrival phase. As soon as the signal from the spacecraft was re-established after a scheduled loss of contact, the Mission Control Team at the European Space Operations Centre (ESOC) in Darmstadt, Germany, saw that the spacecraft had entered safe mode. An issue occurred during the thruster burn, causing the manoeuvre to end earlier than expected. Since then, teams at ESOC have been working around the clock with lead spacecraft manufacturer Airbus and colleagues across the joint ESA and Japan Aerospace Exploration Agency (JAXA) mission to investigate the issue, which has been traced to a software glitch. BepiColombo is safe and in a stable orbit around Mercu
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/bepicolombo-safely-in-mercury-orbit-despite-unexpected-challenge
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 15:10:46 +0100 — Hera’s Didymos asteroid in eclipse
  A solar eclipse in deep space, shown in this early view of the Didymos binary asteroids from the European Space Agency’s Hera mission for planetary defence. The smaller Dimorphos moonlet is seen casting its shadow across part of its Didymos parent body, to blank out the Sun across the shaded stretch of rocky surface.

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . Entre sus competencias clave se encuentran asegurar la acreditaci&oacute;n de seguridad para todas las acciones

- undated — BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto
  BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto &#8211; MEDIA INAF Dopo otto anni e 10,2 miliardi di chilometri percorsi, sabato 21 novembre 2026 la missione dell’Agenzia spaziale europea (Esa) BepiColombo è entrata in orbita attorno a Mercurio. A dare la notizia, un post su X pubblicato dalla stessa agenzia che confermava il completamento della manovra e la ricezione del segnale da parte della sonda. In un post successivo pubblicato il giorno seguente, però, la stessa agenzia ha chiarito che non tutto sarebbe andato come previsto: un&#8217;anomalia software avrebbe provocato l&#8217;interruzione anticipata dell&#8217;accensione dei propulsori, costringendo la sonda a entrare in modalità di sicurezza. In una comunicazione ufficiale pubblicata questa sera, infine, si legge che l’inserimento in orbita è comunque riuscito e che il veicolo si trova in un&#8217;orbita stabile attorno al pia
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/23/bepicolombo-inserimento-in-orbita-con-imprevisto/
- undated — Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai
  Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare. #### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — Space Cowboys
  ## [Een mislukte lancering, een geslaagde landing en heel veel ruimtetelescopen Verder de mijlpaal van één lichtdag van de Voyager 1, een mogelijke nieuwe dwergplaneet - nog zonder naam - en een Chinese missie naar de Jupitermaan Callisto. Dat en meer bespreken Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout en Herbert Blankesteijn in deze nieuwe Space Cowboys. Schrijf je in voor de nieuwsbrief van Space Cowbows en krijg elke aflevering in je mailbox zodra deze uitkomt - op thysroes.nl/spacecowboys SpaceCowboys behandelt ruimtevaart- en astronomienieuws van land, planeet en daarbuiten. Afwisselend gepresenteerd door: Thijs Roes, Herbert Blankesteijn, Michel van Baal, Luc van den Abeelen, Philippe Schoonejans, Erik Laan, Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout, Inge Loes ten Kate, André Kuipers, Bastiaan Bom, Nadine Duursma, Marco Langbroek, Bruno van Wayenburg, Charlotte Pouwels, Marc Heemskerk en Nick Poelstra. - undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 23 november 2026 Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE Lees meer
  Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zeven jaar en vijfhonderd miljoen euro. Dat investeert de Europese Commissie in de ontwikkeling van de ‘digitale tweeling’ Destination Earth. Maar wat kun je met zo’n tweeling? En hoe onderscheidt hij zich van andere informatiediensten? Daarover ging de eerste nationale DestinE Living Lab dag in Amsterdam. &nbsp; Destination Earth ( DestinE ) is een digitale representatie van onze planeet. Een simulatie die de wisselwerking tussen natuur, klimaat en menselijk handelen op onze planeet zo nauwkeurig mogelijk nabootst. Dat doet hij op basis van verschillende databronnen – waaronder satellietdata van het Europese aardobservatieprogramma Copernicus – en met behulp van geavanceerde klimaat- en weermodellen, supercomputers en AI.&nbsp; ‘Satellietgegevens helpen ons te begrijpe
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nieuws/wat-als-betere-besluitvorming-mogelijk-met-digitale-tweeling-destine/

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

Eine Finanzierung kann verschiedene Formen annehmen, darunter direkte Zuschüsse für Forschungsprojekte, Unterstützung für die Entwicklung neuer Technologien und Förderung von Kooperationen zwischen Hochschulen und der Industrie. Mehr über «Raumfahrt: Projektfinanzierungen» Raumfahrt: Business mit der ESA Die ESA lädt Unternehmen aus der Raumfahrtbranche und anderen Branchen ein, sich an ihren Raumfahrtprogrammen zu beteiligen. Die meisten Geschäftsmöglichkeiten mit der ESA werden über ESA STAR publication oder auf dem ESA Star Portal veröffentlicht. Die höheren Fachschulen stellen die Diplomzusätze mit Hilfe von Mustervorlagen des SBFI selbst aus. Nachträgliche Diplomzusätze müssen beim SBFI beantragt werden. Diplomzusätze Sobald ein Abschluss der höheren Berufsbildung im NQR Berufsbildung eingestuft ist, erhalten sämtliche Absolventinnen und Absolventen die Diplomzusätze gemeinsam mit ihren Fachausweisen und Diplomen. Musterdiplomzusätze für die eingestuften Abschüsse stehen im Berufsverzeichnis zur Verfügung. Eidgenössische Prüfungen (eidg. Fachausweis und eidg.   --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ). ### POL

#### Defence24 (press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe&#8217;s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — o

For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Creotech Instruments zwiększa przychody. Znamy wyniki
  Creotech Instruments zwiększa przychody. Znamy wyniki Menu Od stycznia do września 2026 roku Creotech Instruments osiągnął 115,9 mln zł przychodów z działalności kontynuowanej, obejmujących sprzedaż produktów oraz przychody z dotacji. To o ponad 34 mln zł więcej niż w analogicznym okresie ubiegłego roku, kiedy wyniosły one 81,8 mln zł. Sama sprzedaż produktów przyniosła Spółce 111,8 mln zł wobec 77,0 mln zł rok wcześniej. Dodatkowe 4,1 mln zł stanowiły przychody z dotacji. W samym III kwartale 2026 roku przychody ze sprzedaży produktów z działalności kontynuowanej wyniosły 27,7 mln zł, wobec 14,2 mln zł w tym samym okresie 2025 roku. Oznacza to wzrost o ok. 95% rok do roku. Łączne przychody z działalności kontynuowanej osiągnęły w tym okresie 29,3 mln zł, w porównaniu z 15,5 mln zł rok wcześniej. Creotech Instruments rozpoczyna działalność w Niemczech Wynik operacyjny pod wpływem rosnące

- undated — Pięciotonowy człon chińskiej rakiety zbliża się do Ziemi. POLSA monitoruje obiekt
  Pięciotonowy człon chińskiej rakiety zbliża się do Ziemi. POLSA monitoruje obiekt Menu Centrum Operacyjne SSA POLSA poinformowało 26 listopada o monitorowaniu obiektu ZQ-2E R/B , oznaczonego w katalogu NORAD numerem 69095 i identyfikatorem COSPAR 2026-105A. Według danych agencji człon został wyniesiony na orbitę 13 maja 2026 r. z chińskiego kosmodromu Jiuquan. Obiekt jest częścią rakiety Zhuque-2E Block 2 , opracowanej przez chińską firmę LandSpace . Był to lot testowy z ładunkiem symulującym masę satelity o wadze około 2,8 t. Celem misji było sprawdzenie zmodernizowanej wersji rakiety, w tym zmian w konstrukcji pierwszego stopnia i układzie napędowym. Po wyniesieniu ładunku na orbicie pozostał jednak człon rakiety, który obecnie wraca w kierunku Ziemi. Chińska rakieta nad Polską? POLSA monitoruje sytuację Kiedy człon rakiety wejdzie w atmosferę?   Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg. - undated — Slik samarbeider vi med ESA, EU og andre land
  Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. At the NATO Edge defense tech event this past week, NATO announced Starlift, a new multinational space initiative to help secure launch of satellites for military purposes. Ten NATO allies, Belgium, Canada, Finland, Germany, Italy, the Netherlands, Norway, Sweden, Turkey, the United Kingdom, along with and the Republic of Korea, committed to financing the initiative, through the NATO Support and Procurement Agency (NSPA).   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   Kenya hosts regional space law technical advisory mission for Africa News &amp; Updates Kenya hosts Regional Space Law Technical Advisory Mission for Africa &nbsp;The Regional Space Law Technical Advisory Mission for the African Region officially kicked off today at the Luigi Broglio Malindi Space Centre (LBMSC) Malindi, Kenya. Running from 10 to 13 November 2026, the 4-day mission is organized by the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) in collaboration with the Italian Space Agency (ASI) and the Kenya Space Agency (KSA). The event brings together international experts and representatives from African space institutions across 16 nations to strengthen regional capacity in space law and policy. - undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos.   ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine. - undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  About ZINGSA &#8212; Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency Phone +263 8677009885 Email publicrelations@zingsa.ac.zw Hours Mon&#8211;Fri, 8am&#8211;4pm Contact Us About The ZINGSA story Promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial technologies for Zimbabwe&#8217;s development. Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization.   https://zingsa.ac.zw/about.html

ZIMSAT-1 Zimbabwe&#8217;s first satellite, successfully launched in November 2022. Used for monitoring drought conditions, mapping mining activities, assessing crop health, and generating accurate weather forecasts. ZIMSAT-2 Zimbabwe&#8217;s second satellite mission, building on the success of ZIMSAT-1 and expanding national capabilities in space technology and Earth observation. CubeSat Development Advanced CubeSat development using 3D printing technology and modern engineering practices to build indigenous small-satellite capability. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

#### sorae (space_press, ja, feed)

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India.   Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. - undated — Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’
  Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ In Focus Thai military teams up with space agency for ‘eyes in the sky’ MONDAY, SEPTEMBER 05, 2022 | THE NATION สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. Using high-resolution remote sensing data, meteorological data, and a limited set of field measurements, the team mapped annual dry rubber yields from 2021 to 2025. - undated — 又力挺好兄弟黃仁勳，SpaceX 融資 440 億美元要買輝達 Vera Rubin
  又力挺好兄弟黃仁勳，SpaceX 融資 440 億美元要買輝達 Vera Rubin | TechNews 科技新報 在好兄弟黃仁勳回歸輝達擔任執行長之後，特斯拉執行長馬斯克不僅以精神力挺，更以實際的行動支持。旗下的 SpaceX 為了展現其擴張人工智慧（AI）運算版圖的企圖心，由阿波羅全球管理（Apollo Global Management）與高盛（Goldman Sachs）主導的融資交易，已完成約 440 億美元的債務票據定價，資金將用於採購輝達（Nvidia）新一代 Vera Rubin GPU 及相關設備，進一步擴充 SpaceX 的地面AI運算基礎設施。 根據外媒報導，這筆交易採用優先擔保票據及次順位擔保票據等多層次融資結構，並獲得貝萊德（BlackRock）、高盛資產管理及KKR提供投資建議的管理帳戶等機構提供主要投資承諾。由於市場投資需求超出原定規模，最終融資金額進一步擴大。 相關硬體預計於2027年上半年陸續交付，屆時將透過租賃安排提供SpaceX使用，為其AI資料中心及運算服務布局提供重要的硬體支援。 創紀錄的GPU融資，華爾街資金積極進場 此次約440億美元的融資，被Apollo與高盛形容為迄今規模最大的GPU融資交易，反映金融市場對AI運算基礎設施的龐大資金需求。 在交易架構上，Apollo與高盛負責主導融資安排，並透過多層次債務工具募集資金。貝萊德、高盛資產管理及KKR相關管理帳戶則提供主要投資承諾，顯示大型資產管理機構與金融投資者，正積極參與AI基礎建設的資本布局。 Apollo合夥人賈姆希德‧埃薩尼（Jamshid Ehsani）表示，SpaceX是全球最具企圖心的運算基礎設施建置者之一。此次交易結合融資發起、結構設計、銀團籌組及自有資金投資等能力，展現大型金融機構為AI產業提供大規模資金的實力。 值得注意的是，這項交易採取以資產為基礎的融資架構，透過GPU及相關設備做為融資資產，以降低資金提供者面臨的潛在損失風險。 鎖定Nvidia Vera Rubin，擴充地面AI運算能力 本次融資的核心用途，是採購Nvidia新一代Vera Rub
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/27/spacex-raises-44-billion-to-buy-nvidia-and-vera-rubin/

- Sat, 28 Nov 2026 12:22:01 GMT — More than a decade after pilot dream, Global Korea Awards winner defends Korea's skies in F-16s
  Lee Seung-jin won the Model High School Student award at the second Korea Multicultural Youth Award — now known as the Global Korea Award — in 2013. Three years later, he told The Korea Times his dream was to become an Air Force pilot. A decade on, the 29-year-old is living that dream. As a captain with the 19th Fighter Wing, Lee leads a flight of four F-16 fighter jets and helps defend Korea’s airspace every day. “I take pride in every mission because this is a job that not just anyone can do or is trusted with, and because I longed to be in this position,” Lee said in an interview with The Korea Times. - Sat, 28 Nov 2026 12:22:01 GMT — The 4 deaths of Gong Yoo
  This week, I’ve sat in four different classrooms in Seoul, with young adults from all over the world, watching "Train to Busan" as part of our exploration of modern Korean society. A strange choice, you might think, but the messages and critiques are all there bubbling away below the tension created by tight spaces and savage beasts. Students from Kazakhstan cried; Americans whooped, cheered and gesticulated wildly as the movie progressed (Ma Dong-seok’s body generated considerable noise); some Indonesian students ate hamburgers and hid behind their hands; a few Korean students mentioned the continued use of "shinpa" (emotional blackmail) in Korean movies and lamented the gender representation; others told me somewhat proudly they were from Busan. #### RNZ (press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special
  Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special | RNZ Skip to main content space 25 November 2026 Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special From Morning Report , 8:51 am on 25 November 2026 It may have been worth staying up late, or setting an early alarm, to catch a glimpse of the Moon overnight. November's full moon reached 'peak illumination' just a couple of hours ago, and this one is a super moon - meaning it coincides with the Moon being at its closest point to Earth in its orbit. Known as a Beaver Supermoon, it's the first of three consecutive supermoons, with another due in December. So what made last night's moon so special? Astronomer Josh Aoraki spoke to John Campbell. - undated — US senators seek deeper military-space ties with NZ
  US senators seek deeper military-space ties with NZ | RNZ Skip to main content space world 20 November 2026 US senators seek deeper military-space ties with NZ From Morning Report , 7:19 am on 20 November 2026 Nine United States senators have written to the Pentagon calling for deeper military-space ties with New Zealand, including around surveillance and missile defence. A defence consultant says the country should embrace it and not fear retaliation from Russia or China. But a peace activist says this is all about America grooming a South Pacific space outpost. Phil Pennington reports. - undated — Nearly 50 years on, Voyager 1 is about to reach a new milestone
  Nearly 50 years on, Voyager 1 is about to reach a new milestone | RNZ Skip to main content space 16 November 2026 Nearly 50 years on, Voyager 1 is about to reach a new milestone From Nights , 8:35 pm on 16 November 2026 It's almost 50 years since Voyager 1 left earth and it’s now approaching an extraordinary milestone . . . a distance of one light-day from Earth. What began as a mission to explore the outer planets has become one of humanity’s longest-running scientific adventures, with the spacecraft now travelling through interstellar space. And for astrophysicist and University of Auckland Professor of Physics Jan Eldridge, that adventure helped inspire her own career. NASA's Voyager 1 spacecraft is depicted in this artist's concept traveling through interstellar space, or the space between stars, which it entered in 2012.
